However, options 'C' and 'D' are incorrect: hard links cannot span across different partitions, and any modification to data affects both the original file and the hard link, as they are essentially the same file under different names. The student's incorrect response might indicate a misunderstanding of how file systems handle hard links and file structures in the context of the operating system—Linux in this case."
